### Q53. JEE Main 2021 (22 Jul Shift 1)

*Type:* `numerical` | *Difficulty Level:* `2`

Methylation of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>10</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></math> of benzene gave <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>9</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></math> of toluene. Calculate the percentage yield of toluene (Nearest integer)

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **78**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mn>6</mn></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>6</mn></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mi>CH</mi><mn>3</mn></msub><mi>Cl</mi><mo>&#10230;</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mn>6</mn></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>5</mn></msub><msub><mi>CH</mi><mn>3</mn></msub><mo>+</mo><mi>HCl</mi><mspace linebreak="newline"></mspace><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>10</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>gm</mi><mo>&#160;&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mfrac><mn>10</mn><mn>78</mn></mfrac><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>92</mn></mrow></mfenced><mi>gm</mi></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"></math>Percentage yield of toluene = <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi>Actual</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>yield</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>Theoretical</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>yield</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>100</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>9</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mn>920</mn></mfrac><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>78</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>100</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>78</mn><mo>%</mo></math></p>

</details>

### Q54. JEE Main 2021 (20 Jul Shift 1)

An inorganic Compound <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#39;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">X</mi><mo>&#39;</mo></math> on treatment with concentrated <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><msub><mi>SO</mi><mn>4</mn></msub></math> produces brown fumes and gives dark brown ring with <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>FeSO</mi><mn>4</mn></msub></math> in presence of concentrated <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><msub><mi>SO</mi><mn>4</mn></msub></math>. Also Compound <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#39;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">X</mi><mo>&#39;</mo></math> gives precipitate <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#39;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">Y</mi><mo>&#39;</mo></math>, when its solution in dilute <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>HCl</mi></math> is treated with <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">S</mi></math> gas. The precipitate <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#39;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">Y</mi><mo>&#39;</mo></math> on treatment with concentrated <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>HNO</mi><mn>3</mn></msub></math> followed by excess of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>NH</mi><mn>4</mn></msub><mi>OH</mi></math> further gives deep blue coloured solution, Compound <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#39;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">X</mi><mo>&#39;</mo></math> is:

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Co</mi><msub><mfenced><msub><mi>NO</mi><mn>3</mn></msub></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msub></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Pb</mi><msub><mfenced><msub><mi>NO</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msub></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Cu</mi><msub><mfenced><msub><mi>NO</mi><mn>3</mn></msub></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msub></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Pb</mi><msub><mfenced><msub><mi>NO</mi><mn>3</mn></msub></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msub></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Cu</mi><msub><mfenced><msub><mi>NO</mi><mn>3</mn></msub></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msub></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

### Q55. JEE Main 2021 (18 Mar Shift 2)

*Type:* `numerical` | *Difficulty Level:* `1`

<p><img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/76f96904-9539-4267-91d9-d9a9669cf502-image.png" style="width: 337px; height: 113px;" /></p><p>Consider the above reaction where <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>6</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></math> of benzoic acid is used to get <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>7</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>8</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></math> of m-bromo benzoic acid. The percentage yield of the product is ___ .</p><p>(Round off to the Nearest integer)</p><p>[Given : Atomic masses : <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>12</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mo>:</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mo>:</mo><mn>16</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>Br</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>80</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi></math>]</p>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **78**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Moles of Benzoic acid <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>6</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>122</mn></mfrac></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo></math> moles of m-bromobenzoic acid</p><p>So, weight of m-bromobenzoic acid</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>6</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>122</mn></mfrac><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>201</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>gm</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mn>10</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>05</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>gm</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>%</mo></math> yield <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>Actual</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>weight</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>Theoretical</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>weight</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>100</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>7</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>8</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>10</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>05</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>100</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mn>77</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>61</mn><mo>%</mo></math></p>

</details>

### Q56. JEE Main 2021 (18 Mar Shift 1)

*Type:* `numerical` | *Difficulty Level:* `1`

Complete combustion of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>3</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></math> of ethane gives <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mn>10</mn><mn>22</mn></msup><mtext>molecules</mtext></math> of water. The value of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></math> is ______ (Round off to the Nearest Integer).
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="[" close="]"><mrow><mtext>Use</mtext><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>:</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>023</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mn>10</mn><mn>23</mn></msup><mo>;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mtext>Atomic&#160;masses&#160;in</mtext><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi><mo>:</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mo>:</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>12</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mo>:</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>16</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mo>:</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **18**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>6</mn></msub><mo>+</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mfrac><mn>7</mn><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>&#160;</mo></mrow></mfrac><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>&#8594;</mo><mn>2</mn><msub><mi>CO</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>+</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>3</mn><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>MW</mi><mrow><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>6</mn></msub></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>30</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>30</mn><mi>g</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>o</mi><mi>f</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>6</mn></msub></math>  gives rise to <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>3</mn></math> moles of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>3</mn><mi>g</mi><mo>&#160;</mo></math>of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>6</mn></msub></math> gives rise to <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>0</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>&#160;</mo></math>moles of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></math></p><p>No. of molecules <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>023</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mn>10</mn><mn>23</mn></msup></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mn>18</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>069</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mn>10</mn><mn>22</mn></msup></math></p>

</details>

Hence, the percentage yield of product.</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>210</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>272</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>100</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>77</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>20</mn><mo>&#8776;</mo><mn>77</mn></math></p><p>OR</p><p><img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/e74e35f6-bd7c-4eb2-9563-72448541458a-image.png" style="width: 392px; <p><img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/8cc3bec3-f897-4ca7-b33b-0a5a3ad55018-image.png" style="width: 263px; height: 134px;" /></p><p>In the above reaction, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>3</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>9</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></math> of benzene on nitration gives <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>4</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>92</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></math> of nitrobenzene. The percentage yield of nitrobenzene in the above reaction is _________ <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>%</mo></math>. (Round off to the Nearest Integer).</p><p>(Given atomic mass : <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>:</mo><mn>12</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>:</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi><mo>&#160;</mo></math><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>:</mo><mn>16</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>:</mo><mn>14</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi></math>)</p>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **80**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/4d3a1c64-a3eb-411d-977c-75a16052346b-image.png" style="width: 256px; height: 181px;" /></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>3</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>9</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>gm</mi><mo>&#160;&#160;</mo><mfrac><mn>123</mn><mn>78</mn></mfrac><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>9</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>15</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>gm</mi></math></p><p>But actual amount of nitrobenzene formed is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>4</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>92</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>gm</mi></math> and hence.</p><p>Percentage yield<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>92</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>15</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>100</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>80</mn><mo>%</mo></math></p>

</details>

### Q66. JEE Main 2020 (02 Sep Shift 2)

*Type:* `numerical` | *Difficulty Level:* `3`

**Correct Value:** **5**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p> Mass ratio of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>:</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>is</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>:</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>12</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>:</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>&#160;</mo></math></p><p>&amp; <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>:</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>is</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>:</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#8658;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>12</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>:</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>16</mn><mo>&#160;</mo></math></p><p>so <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>mass</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>mole</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>mole</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>ratio</mi><mspace linebreak="newline"></mspace><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>12</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>1</mn><mspace linebreak="newline"></mspace><mi formula <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo></math><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>CH</mi><mn>3</mn></msub><mi  <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn></math>mole       <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>5</mn></math>mole</p><p>so required moles of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>&#160;</mo></math>is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>5</mn></math></p>

### Q67. JEE Main 2020 (07 Jan Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Difficulty Level:* `2`

The ammonia <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced></math> released on quantitative reaction of <math><mn>0.6&#160;</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></math> urea <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced></math> with sodium hydroxide <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> can be neutralized by

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>200</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></math> of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>0.4</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>200</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></math> of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>0.2</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>1</mn><mn>00</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></math> of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>0.2</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>100</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></math> of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>0.1</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>1</mn><mn>00</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></math> of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>0.2</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>NH</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><msub><mi>CONH</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>+</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>NaOH</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#8594;</mo><mn>2</mn><msub><mi>NH</mi><mn>3</mn></msub><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>+</mo><msub><mi>Na</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><msub><mi>CO</mi><mn>3</mn></msub></math></p><p>By, stoichiometry, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>1</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>mol</mi></math> of urea gives <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>mol</mi></math> <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>NH</mi><mn>3</mn></msub></math></p><p>Moles of urea =<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>gm</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>60</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>gm</mi><mo>/</mo><mi>mol</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>01</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>mol</mi></math></p><p>So, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>0</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>01</mn></math> mole urea produce <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>0</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>02</mn></math> mole <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>NH</mi><mn>3</mn></msub></math></p><p>To neutralize ammonia <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>HCl</mi></math> is required</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>NH</mi><mn>3</mn></msub><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>+</mo><mi>HCl</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><msub><mi>NH</mi><mn>4</mn></msub><mi>Cl</mi></math></p><p>By equation, it is clear that <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>1</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>mol</mi></math> of ammonia is neutralized by 1mol of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>HCl</mi></math>.</p><p>So, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>0</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>02</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>mol</mi></math> of ammonia neutralized by <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>0</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>02</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>mol</mi></math> of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>HCl</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo><mi>Mo</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi>e&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi>f&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0.02&#160;</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Equivalent</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>of</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>HCl</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>normality</mi><mo>&#215;</mo><mi>volume</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>100</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>mL</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>02</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>equivalent</mi></math></p><p>As <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></math>-factor of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>HCl</mi></math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>1</mn></math>, so <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>number</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>of</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>equivalents</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>number</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>of</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>moles</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>02</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>moles</mi></math></p><p>Hence, option <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>3</mn></math> is correct.</p>

</details>

<math><mn>25</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></math> of an unknown hydrocarbon upon burnig produces <math><mn>88</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></math> of <math><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math> and <math><mn>9</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></math> of <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mo>.</mo></math> This unknown hydrocarbon contains:

- **(B)** <math><mn>18</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></math> of carbon annd <math><mn>7</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></math> of hydrogen
- **(C)** <math><mn>22</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></math> of carbon and <math><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></math> of hydrogen
- **(D)** <math><mn>20</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></math> carbon and <math><mn>5</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></math> of hydrogen

**Detailed Solution:**

<img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/4b188dd8-6a1b-42fc-b9dd-1d10a23f18c7-image.png" style="width: 400px; height: 166px;" />
<math><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></math> of <math><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math> contains <math><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></math> of carbon, Hence weight of carbon <math><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>12</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>24</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></math> .
mass of Hydrogen <math><mo>=</mo><mn>25</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>24</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math>

</details>

### Q69. JEE Main 2019 (10 Apr Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Difficulty Level:* `3`

At <math><mn>300</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></math> and <math><mn>1</mn></math> atmospheric pressure, <math><mn>10</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></math> of a hydrocarbon required <math><mn>55</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi></math> of <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math> for complete combustion, and <math><mn>40</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></math> of <math><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math> is formed. The formula of the hydrocarbon is:

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msub><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>8</mn></mrow></msub></math>
- **(B)** <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msub><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow></msub></math>
- **(C)** <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msub><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></msub></math>
- **(D)** <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msub><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>7</mn></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msub><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></msub></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Let hydrocarbon is <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></msub><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi></mrow></msub></math>
<img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/dc409275-f590-475f-bd8b-bac28464d4b8-image16.png" />
<math><mn>10</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>40</mn></math> ; <math><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></math> and <math><mn>10</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>55</mn></math>
<math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>55</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>5.5</mn></math>
Put the value of <math><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></math>
<math><mn>4</mn><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>5.5</mn></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mn>4</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mn>4</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>5</mn></math> then <math><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>6</mn></math>
Hence hydrocarbon is <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msub><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></msub></math>

</details>

### Q70. JEE Main 2019 (09 Apr Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Difficulty Level:* `2`

For a reaction,
<math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>&#10230;</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>)</mo></math>, identify di-hydrogen <math><mo>(</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>)</mo></math> as a limiting reagent in the following reaction mixtures.

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>28</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><mn>6</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>35</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><mn>8</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>56</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><mn>10</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>14</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><mn>56</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><mn>10</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><math><msub><mrow><mtext>N</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8594;</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mtext>moles&#160;of&#160;</mtext><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></mrow><mrow><mtext>stoichiometry&#160;of&#160;</mtext><msub><mtext>N</mtext><mn>2</mn></msub></mrow></mfrac><mo>&#8800;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mtext>mole&#160;of&#160;</mtext><msub><mtext>H</mtext><mn>2</mn></msub></mrow><mrow><mtext>stoichiometry&#160;of&#160;</mtext><msub><mtext>H</mtext><mn>2</mn></msub></mrow></mfrac></math>
Whose ratio is less called limiting reagent.</p><p>
(a) mole of <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>28</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>28</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
<math><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">h</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi></mrow></mfrac></math> ratio of <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
mole of <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math>
<math><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">h</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi></mrow></mfrac></math> ratio of <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
there is no limiting reagent.</p><p>
(b) mole of <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>35</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>28</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>1.25</mn></math>
<math><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">h</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi></mrow></mfrac></math> ratio of <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1.25</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> limiting reagent
mole of <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>8</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></math>
<math><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">h</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi></mrow></mfrac></math> ratio of <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>1.33</mn></math></p><p>
(c) mole of <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>56</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>28</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>
<math><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">h</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi></mrow></mfrac></math> ratio of <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>
mole of <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn></math>
<math><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">h</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi></mrow></mfrac></math> ratio of <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>1.66</mn></math> limiting reagent.</p><p>
(d) mole of <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>14</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>28</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>0.5</mn></math>
<math><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">h</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi></mrow></mfrac></math> ratio of <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>0.5</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>0.5</mn></math> limiting reagent
mole of <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>
<math><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">h</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi></mrow></mfrac></math> ratio of <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>1.66</mn></math></p><p><math><mo>&#8756;</mo><mtext>&#8201;</mtext></math> Answer is <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> .</p>

</details>

### Q71. JEE Main 2019 (08 Apr Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Difficulty Level:* `3`

<math><mn>0.27</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></math> of a long chain fatty acid was dissolved in <math><mn>100</mn><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></math> of hexane. <math><mn>10</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi></math> of this solution was added dropwise to the surface of water in a round watch glass. Hexane evaporates and a monolayer is formed. The distance from edge to centre of the watch glass is <math><mn>10</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math> . What is the height of the monolayer?
[Density of fatty acid <math><mo>=</mo><mn>0.9</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mo>;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>]</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi></math>

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math>
- **(C)** <math><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math>
- **(D)** <math><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Mass of fatty acid <math><mo>=</mo><mn>0.027</mn><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></math> in <math><mn>10</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></math> solution</p><p>Density of fatty acid <math><mn>0.9</mn><mtext>&#8201;</mtext></math> g/cc</p><p>Volume of fatty acid <math><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>0.027</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>0.9</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>0.03</mn><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></math></p><p>Area of plate <math><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>300</mn><mtext>&#8201;&#8201;</mtext><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math></p><p>Height of fatty acid layer <math><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">v</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>0.03</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>300</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math> = <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mn>10</mn><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math></p>

</details>

### Q72. JEE Main 2019 (09 Jan Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Difficulty Level:* `1`

For the following reaction, the mass of water produced from <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>445</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi></math> of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mn>57</mn></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>110</mn></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mn>6</mn></msub></math> is: <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mn>57</mn></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>110</mn></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mn>6</mn></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>163</mn><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mfenced><mo>&#8594;</mo><mn>114</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><msub><mi>CO</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>110</mn><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mn>l</mn></mfenced></math>

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>490</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>890</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>445</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>495</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>495</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><msub><mrow><mi>C</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>57</mn></mrow></msub><msub><mrow><mi>H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>110</mn></mrow></msub><msub><mrow><mi>O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>163</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><msub><mrow><mi>O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>&#8594;</mo><mn>57</mn><mi>C</mi><msub><mrow><mi>O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><mn>55</mn><msub><mrow><mi>H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mi>O</mi></math>

Molecular weight of <math><msub><mrow><mi>C</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>57</mn></mrow></msub><msub><mrow><mi>H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>110</mn></mrow></msub><msub><mrow><mi>O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>890</mn></math>

Moles of <math><msub><mrow><mi>C</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>57</mn></mrow></msub><msub><mrow><mi>H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>110</mn></mrow></msub><msub><mrow><mi>O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">w</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">h</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">w</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">h</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>

<math><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>445</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>890</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

According to stoichiometry

<math><mo>&#8757;</mo><mn>1</mn><mi>&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi>&#160;</mi></math> of <math><msub><mrow><mi>C</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>57</mn></mrow></msub><msub><mrow><mi>H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>110</mn></mrow></msub><msub><mrow><mi>O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></msub></math> gives <math><mo>=</mo><mn>55</mn><mi>&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></math> of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>H</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mi>O</mi></math>

<math><mo>&#8757;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi>&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi>&#160;</mi></math> of <math><msub><mrow><mi>C</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>57</mn></mrow></msub><msub><mrow><mi>H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>110</mn></mrow></msub><msub><mrow><mi>O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></msub></math> gives <math><mo>=</mo><mn>55</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi>&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></math> of <math><msub><mrow><mi>H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mi>O</mi></math>

Wt. of <math><msub><mrow><mi>H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mi>O</mi><mo>=</mo></math> mole of <math><msub><mrow><mi>H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mi>O</mi><mo>&#215;</mo><mi>M</mi></math> Wt. of <math><msub><mrow><mi>H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mi>O</mi></math>

<math><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>55</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>18</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>495</mn><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>g</mi><mi>m</mi></math>

</details>

### Q73. JEE Main 2018 (16 Apr Online)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Difficulty Level:* `2`

An unknown chlorohydrocarbon has 3.55 % of chlorine. If each molecule of the hydrocarbon has one chlorine atom only; chlorine atoms present in 1 g of chlorohydrocarbon are :(Atomic wt. of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Cl</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>35</mn><mo>.5</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi><mo>;</mo></math> Avogadro constant <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>.023</mo><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mn>10</mn><mn>23</mn></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><msup><mi>mol</mi><mrow><mo>&#8211;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>)

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>6.023</mn><mi> </mi><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>9</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>6.023</mn><mi> </mi><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>23</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>6.023</mn><mi> </mi><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>21</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>6.023</mn><mi> </mi><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>20</mn></mrow></msup></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math><mn>6.023</mn><mi> </mi><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>20</mn></mrow></msup></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi></msub><mi>Cl</mi></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>%&#160;</mo><mi>Cl</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>.55</mo></math>

Weight of <math><mi>C</mi><mi>l</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mi>&#160;</mi><mo>&#215;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3.55</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>100</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<math><msub><mrow><mi>n</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>C</mi><msup><mrow><mi>l</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn><mi>&#160;</mi><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>3.55</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>100</mn><mi>&#160;</mi><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>35.5</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>No</mi><mo>.</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi>of</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><msup><mi>Cl</mi><mo>-</mo></msup><mi>ion</mi><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mo>&#215;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mn>3</mn><mo>.55</mo></mrow><mrow><mn>100</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>35</mn><mo>.5</mo></mrow></mfrac><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>.023</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mn>10</mn><mn>23</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mn>6</mn><mo>.023</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mn>10</mn><mn>20</mn></msup></math>

</details>

### Q74. JEE Main 2018 (15 Apr Shift 2 Online)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Difficulty Level:* `2`

For per gram of reactant, the maximum quantity of $\mathrm{N}_2$ gas is produced in which of the following thermal decomposition reactions? (Given: Atomic wt. : $\mathrm{Cr}=52 \mathrm{u}, \mathrm{Ba}=137 \mathrm{u}$ ).

**Options:**

- **(A)** $\mathrm{Ba}\left(\mathrm{N}_3\right)_2$ (s) $\longrightarrow \mathrm{Ba}(\mathrm{C})+3 \mathrm{~N}_2(\mathrm{~g})$
- **(B)** $\left(\mathrm{NH}_4\right)_2 \mathrm{Cr}_2 \mathrm{O}_7(\mathrm{~s}) \longrightarrow$
$$
\mathrm{N}_2(\mathrm{~g})+4 \mathrm{H}_2 \mathrm{O}(\mathrm{g})+\mathrm{Cr}_2 \mathrm{O}_3(\mathrm{~s})
$$
- **(C)** $2 \mathrm{NH}_3(\mathrm{~g}) \longrightarrow \mathrm{N}_2(\mathrm{~g})+3 \mathrm{H}_2(\mathrm{~g})$
- **(D)** $2 \mathrm{NH}_4 \mathrm{NO}_3(\mathrm{~s}) \longrightarrow$
$$
2 \mathrm{~N}_2(\mathrm{~g})+4 \mathrm{H}_2 \mathrm{O}(\mathrm{g})+\mathrm{O}_2(\mathrm{~g})
$$

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) $2 \mathrm{NH}_3(\mathrm{~g}) \longrightarrow \mathrm{N}_2(\mathrm{~g})+3 \mathrm{H}_2(\mathrm{~g})$**

**Detailed Solution:**

(a) Molar mass of Ba $\left(\mathrm{N}_3\right)_2(\mathrm{~s})=221 \mathrm{~g} / \mathrm{mol}$ 1 mole of $\mathrm{Ba}\left(\mathrm{N}_3\right)_2(\mathrm{~s})$ will give 3 moles of $\mathrm{N}_2$ hence $\frac{1 \mathrm{~g}}{221 \mathrm{~g} / \mathrm{mol}}$ moles of $\mathrm{Ba}\left(\mathrm{N}_3\right)_2(\mathrm{~s})$ will give $3 \times \frac{1}{221}$ $=0.014$ moles of $\mathrm{N}_2$
(b) Molar mass of $\left(\mathrm{NH}_4\right)_2 \mathrm{Cr}_2 \mathrm{O}_7=252 \mathrm{~g} / \mathrm{mol}$. 1 mole of $\left(\mathrm{NH}_4\right)_2 \mathrm{Cr}_2 \mathrm{O}_7$ will give 1 mole of $\mathrm{N}_2$ hence $\frac{1 \mathrm{~g}}{252 \mathrm{~g} / \mathrm{mol}}$ moles of $\left(\mathrm{NH}_4\right)_2 \mathrm{Cr}_2 \mathrm{O}_7$ will give $1 \times \frac{1}{252}=0.0039$ moles of $\mathrm{N}_2$
(c) Molar mass of $\mathrm{NH}_3=17 \mathrm{~g} / \mathrm{mol}$. 2 mole of $\mathrm{NH}_3$ will give 1 mole of $\mathrm{N}_2$
hence $\frac{1 \mathrm{~g}}{17 \mathrm{~g} / \mathrm{mol}}$ moles of $\mathrm{NH}_3$ will give
$$
\frac{1}{2 \times 17}=0.0297 \text { moles of } \mathrm{N}_2 \text {. }
$$
(d) Molar mass of $\mathrm{NH}_4 \mathrm{NO}_3=80 \mathrm{~g} / \mathrm{mol}$. 1 mole of $\mathrm{NH}_4 \mathrm{NO}_3$ will give 1 mole of $\mathrm{N}_2$ hence $\frac{1 \mathrm{~g}}{80 \mathrm{~g} / \mathrm{mol}}$ moles $\mathrm{NH}_4 \mathrm{NO}_3$ will give
$$
1 \times \frac{1}{80}=0.0125 \text { moles of } \mathrm{N}_2
$$
Hence Thermal decomposition of $\mathrm{NH}_3$ will produce maximum amount of $\mathrm{N}_2$.

</details>

### Q75. JEE Main 2018 (08 Apr)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Difficulty Level:* `2`

The ratio of mass percent of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>and</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></math> of an organic compound <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">X</mi></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">Y</mi></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">Z</mi></msub></mrow></mfenced></math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>6</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>:</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>.</mo></math> If one molecule of the above compound <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">X</mi></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">Y</mi></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">Z</mi></msub></mrow></mfenced></math> contains half as much oxygen as required to burn one molecule of compound <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">X</mi></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">Y</mi></msub></math> completely to <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>CO</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mo>.</mo></math> The empirical formula of the compound <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">X</mi></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">Y</mi></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">Z</mi></msub></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>4</mn></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mn>3</mn></msub></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mn>3</mn></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>6</mn></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mn>3</mn></msub></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>4</mn></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mn>3</mn></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>4</mn></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>4</mn></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mn>3</mn></msub></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Mass of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></math>: Mass of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>:</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>1</mn></math>.
Or, for every atom of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>(</mo><mn>12</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>,</mo></math> we have <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mo>&#160;</mo></math>atoms of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>(</mo><mn>1</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#215;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi><mo>)</mo></math>
Thus, the ratio of atoms of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>:</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>:</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>:</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>2</mn></math>
Thus, the formula is: <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></msub></math>
Combustion of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></msub></math>:
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><mo>(</mo><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>/</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>)</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>&#8594;</mo><msub><mi>xCO</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mi>xH</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></math>
Hence, the combustion of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></msub></math> requires <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>/</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>)</mo></math> oxygen molecules.</p><p>Hence, the original molecule contains <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>/</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>)</mo><mo>&#160;</mo></math>molecules <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mo>(</mo><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>/</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>)</mo><mo>&#160;</mo></math>atoms.
Or, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>/</mo><mn>2</mn></math></p><p>Empirical Formula<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>/</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>4</mn></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mn>3</mn></msub></math></p>

</details>

### Q76. JEE Main 2016 (03 Apr)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Difficulty Level:* `2`

At <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>300</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>1</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>atm</mi></math>, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>15</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>mL</mi></math> of a gaseous hydrocarbon requires <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>375</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>mL</mi></math> air containing <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>20</mn><mo>%</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></math> by volume, for complete combustion. After combustion, the gases occupy <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>345</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>mL</mi></math>. Assuming that the water formed is in liquid form and the volumes were measured at the same temperature and pressure, the formula of the hydrocarbon is:
(Assume complete combustion of reactant)

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mn>4</mn></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>8</mn></msub></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mn>4</mn></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>10</mn></msub></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mn>3</mn></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>6</mn></msub></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mn>3</mn></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>8</mn></msub></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mn>3</mn></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>8</mn></msub></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Volume of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></math> in air<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mn>375</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>0.8</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>300</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi>ml</mi></math>
Volume of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></math> in air<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mn>375</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>0.2</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>75</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi>ml</mi></math>
<math> <mrow> <mtable> <mtr> <mtd> <msub> <mtext>C</mtext> <mtext>x</mtext> </msub> <msub> <mtext>H</mtext> <mtext>y</mtext> </msub> </mtd> <mtd> <mo>+</mo> </mtd> <mtd> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>x</mtext> <mo>+</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mtext>y</mtext> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>4</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </mfenced> <msub> <mtext>O</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msub> </mtd> <mtd> <mo>&#10230;</mo> </mtd> <mtd> <msub> <mtext>xCO</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msub> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>g</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> </mtd> <mtd> <mo>+</mo> </mtd> <mtd> <mfrac> <mrow> <mtext>y</mtext> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> <msub> <mtext>H</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msub> <mtext>O</mtext> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mi>&#8467;</mi> </mrow> </mfenced> </mtd> </mtr> <mtr> <mtd> <mn>1</mn> <mn>5</mn> <mtext>ml</mtext> </mtd> <mtd> </mtd> <mtd> <mn>1</mn> <mn>5</mn> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>x</mtext> <mo>+</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mtext>y</mtext> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>4</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </mfenced> </mtd> <mtd> </mtd> <mtd> </mtd> <mtd> </mtd> <mtd> </mtd> </mtr> <mtr> <mtd> <mn>0</mn> </mtd> <mtd> </mtd> <mtd> <mn>0</mn> </mtd> <mtd> </mtd> <mtd> <mn>1</mn> <mn>5</mn> <mtext>x</mtext> </mtd> <mtd> </mtd> <mtd> <mo>-</mo> </mtd> </mtr> </mtable> </mrow> </math>
After combustion, total volume
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>345</mn><mo>=</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi><msub><mi>CO</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></msub></math>
<math><mn>345</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>300</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>15</mn><mi>x</mi></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math>
Volume of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></math> used
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>15</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mn>4</mn></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>75</mn></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mn>4</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>8</mn></math>
So, hydrocarbon is<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mn>3</mn></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>8</mn></msub></math>.

</details>

### Q77. JEE Main 2015 (11 Apr Online)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Difficulty Level:* `1`

An element <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">X</mi></math> shows <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn></math>, oxidation state in its compounds. Out of the four compounds given below, choose the incorrect formula for the element <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">X</mi></math>.

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">X</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></math>
- **(B)** <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">X</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><msub><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">S</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></math>
- **(C)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">X</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msub></math>
- **(D)** <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">X</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">X</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Oxidation state of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">X</mi></math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn></math>. The formula should be <math><mi mathvariant="normal">X</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></math> and not <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">X</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></math>.

</details>

### Q78. JEE Main 2015 (10 Apr Online)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Difficulty Level:* `2`

A sample of a hydrate of barium chloride weighing 61 g was heated until all the water of hydration is removed. The dried sample weighed 52 g. The formula of the hydrated salt is: (atomic mass, Ba = 137 amu, Cl = 35.5 amu)

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>&sdot;</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>&sdot;</mo><msub><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></math>
- **(C)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>&sdot;</mo><msub><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></math>
- **(D)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>&sdot;</mo><mn>2</mn><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>&sdot;</mo><mn>2</mn><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>&sdot;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mo>→</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi></math> 

                 <math><mo>(</mo><mn>137</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>35.5</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>18</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>)</mo></math> 

                 <math><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>208</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>18</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi></math> g/mole

                 <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>208</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mn>18</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>208</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>61</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>52</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> 

                 <math><mn>10816</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>936</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>12688</mn></math> 

                 <math><mn>936</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1872</mn></math> 

                 <math><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math> 

                Formula is  <math><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>&sdot;</mo><mn>2</mn><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></math>

</details>

### Q79. JEE Main 2014 (12 Apr Online)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Difficulty Level:* `2`

The amount of $\mathrm{BaSO}_4$ formed upon mixing 100 $\mathrm{mL}$ of $20.8 \% \mathrm{BaCl}_2$ solution with $50 \mathrm{~mL}$ of $9.8 \%$ $\mathrm{H}_2 \mathrm{SO}_4$ solution will be: $(\mathrm{Ba}=137, \mathrm{Cl}=35.5, \mathrm{~S}=32, \mathrm{H}=1$ and $\mathrm{O}=16$ )

**Options:**

- **(A)** $23.3 \mathrm{~g}$
- **(B)** $11.65 \mathrm{~g}$
- **(C)** $30.6 \mathrm{~g}$
- **(D)** $33.2 \mathrm{~g}$

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) $23.3 \mathrm{~g}$**

**Detailed Solution:**

$\mathrm{BaCl}_2+\mathrm{H}_2 \mathrm{SO}_4 \longrightarrow \mathrm{BaSO}_4+2 \mathrm{HCl}$
$\begin{array}{llll}208 \mathrm{~g} & 98 \mathrm{~g} & 233 \mathrm{~g} & 73 \mathrm{~g} \\ 20.8 \mathrm{~g} & 9.8 \mathrm{~g} & 23.3 \mathrm{~g} & 7.3 \mathrm{~g}\end{array}$

</details>

### Q80. JEE Main 2014 (11 Apr Online)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Difficulty Level:* `2`

A gaseous compound of nitrogen and hydrogen contains $12.5 \%$ (by mass) of hydrogen. The density of the compound relative to hydrogen is 16. The molecular formula of the compound is:

**Options:**

- **(A)** $\mathrm{NH}_2$
- **(B)** $\mathrm{N}_3 \mathrm{H}$
- **(C)** $\mathrm{NH}_3$
- **(D)** $\mathrm{N}_2 \mathrm{H}_4$

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) $\mathrm{N}_2 \mathrm{H}_4$**

**Detailed Solution:**

In an unknown compounds containing $\mathrm{N}$ and $\mathrm{H}$
given $\%$ of $\mathrm{H}=12.5 \%$
$$
\therefore \% \text { of } N=100-12.5=87.5 \%
$$

$$
\begin{array}{|c|c|c|c|}
\hline \text { Element } & \text { Percentage } & \text { Atomic ratio } & \text { Simple ratio } \\
\hline \mathrm{H} & 12.5 \% & \frac{12.5}{1}=12.5 & \frac{12.5}{6.25}=2 \\
\hline \mathrm{N} & 87.5 & \frac{87.5}{14}=6.25 & \frac{6.25}{6.25}=1 \\
\hline
\end{array}
$$

$2 \times$ vapour density $=$ Mol. wt $=16 \times 2=32$.
Molecular formula $=n \times$ empirical formula mass
$$
n=\frac{32}{16}=2
$$
$\therefore$ Molecular formula of the compound will be $=\left(\mathrm{NH}_2\right)_2$ $=\mathrm{N}_2 \mathrm{H}_4$

</details>
